# Display-render catalogue work

## Fenghua 0402 and 0603 Y5V secondary evidence (2026-11-18)

Published three Y5V 50 V pages: 0402 22 nF, 0603 12 nF and 0603 150 nF.
Identity, geometry, dielectric, provenance and image checks passed. Secondary
listings support dimensions and ratings; original manufacturer drawings were
not independently accessed, disclosed on pages. Prior files are backed up
under `tmp/display_render/fenghua_y5v_secondary_previous/`. No PDF was
downloaded. There are now 231 published capacitor pages; remaining capacitors
and later families need review.

## Fenghua 0603 25 V X7R (2026-11-18)

Published 220 nF and 330 nF pages. Identity, geometry, 25 V rating,
provenance and image checks passed. The 220 nF dimensions and ratings come

not independently accessed; Fenghua's catalogue supports the 330 nF part.
Evidence levels are disclosed on the pages. The older 0603 source manifest
omits a non-finite pixel count. Prior files are backed up under
`tmp/display_render/fenghua_0603_25v_previous/`. No PDF was downloaded.
There are now 228 published capacitor pages; remaining capacitors and later
families need review.

## Fenghua 0603 8.2 pF and 1206 height discrepancy (2026-11-18)

Published the 0603 8.2 pF page. Identity, geometry, voltage, provenance and
image checks passed. A secondary supplier supports its dimensions and ratings;
the manufacturer drawing was not independently accessed, and the source
record omits tolerance, both disclosed. Prior files are backed up under
`tmp/display_render/fenghua_0603_8p2_secondary_previous/`. No PDF was downloaded.
There are now 209 published capacitor pages.

Follow-up: a secondary listing for 1206CG180J500NT reports 1.60 mm height
(https://m.elecfans.com/article/xc-2500236407.html) while the manufacturer
catalogue reports 0.80 mm (https://www.fhcomp.com/zh-cn/product_page/?code=010121&page=157).
The published 18 pF page uses manufacturer-listed 0.80 mm. Similar secondary
1.60 mm claims for unrendered 1206CG101J500NT and 1206CG120J500NT require
individual manufacturer verification before selecting a height. Remaining
capacitors and later families need review.

## Array research for next pass

KiCad contains valid `R_Array_Convex_4x0402.step` and `R_Array_Convex_4x0603.step`
models. Their inspected SHA-256 hashes are respectively
`36ded1f7b7c9e9dcddde3eb3200acdf1c0e0b60046893660e6f043afc2ec7fad` and
`bec072c4d44e4c5ff693e18ede4078c7e66e7a670d53b00a20edd2c0aecdf3c2`; bounds
are 1 × 2 × 0.35 mm and 1.6 × 3.2 × 0.5 mm respectively. They have coating,

Fenghua's manufacturer-authored RC-M network datasheet was inspected online:
https://szsdmdz.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/12/%E9%A3%8E%E5%8D%8E%E9%AB%98%E7%A7%91-%E5%8E%9A%E8%86%9C%E7%89%87%E5%BC%8F%E7%BD%91%E7%BB%9C%E7%94%B5%E9%98%BB%E5%99%A8-7.0.pdf
It describes RC-MT08W as a four-resistor, eight-terminal convex array, gives
nominal 2 × 1 × 0.45 mm dimensions (the KiCad height is at the tolerance
limit), shows a marked top and gives the value-code/decimal convention. Do not
apply the unmarked rule for standalone 0402 resistors to these arrays. Verify
the top-face layout, text orientation, pin appearance and individual source
records before developing and reviewing an array-specific render adapter. No
local PDF has been captured.

A suitable gold-band candidate for the next rule is the catalogue's 6.8 kΩ
YAGEO CFR-25JB-52-6K8, specified as 5% in its source record. The
manufacturer-authored CFR specification (reviewed online only) documents

## RALEC RTT05/06 batch (2026-11-17)

Published 21 reviewed RALEC 0805 and 1206 resistor renders and individual
README updates. Their exact primary part numbers, values, tolerance, decimal
or exponent marking format and footprint are checked by
`display_render_ralec_rtt_queue.py`. The reviewed RALEC 2011 marking table
and 2024 dimensions are linked in `DISPLAY_RENDER_RALEC_RTT_EVIDENCE.md`;
the PDFs were read online and were not downloaded. The shared-pass render
approximation is disclosed in each output. Manufacturer 1206 body length is
3.05 ± 0.10 mm, while the generic KiCad display model is 3.20 mm; the
discrepancy is explicitly stated in those component descriptions. The ID
`electronic_resistor_0805_1_ohm_uni_royal_uniroyal_elec_rtt051r00ftp`
claims UNI-ROYAL in its taxonomy but RALEC in its source; it was not changed
or rendered and needs an identity review. The focused suite passed 29 tests.
All 430 resistor recipes currently pass render provenance checks and have
README image references. Continue researching remaining resistor entries,
then capacitors and ICs in the requested order.

## TAJ A-case 10 µF 10 V display pilot and polarity correction (2026-11-18)

Published TAJA106K010RNJ with an inspected 1400 px yellow KiCad tantalum
render, metal terminations, positive-anode stripe and illustrative `106 A`
marking. KiCad Kemet A-case geometry is scaled to the manufacturer nominal
1.6 mm height. The manufacturer permits higher-rated substitutions, so actual
marking, logo, production code and finishes can differ, disclosed on the page.
The renderer uses a purpose-specific tantalum material and bounded text offset;
the previous renderer is archived by hash. Corrected an incorrect negative
stripe note for this pilot, 56 other TAJ source records and their generated
metadata, and all 57 corresponding populate definitions. Other capacitor types
were left untouched. Backups are under `tmp/display_render/taj_a_pilot_previous/`
and `tmp/display_render/taj_polarity_corrections_previous/`. Validated 56 changed
parts and 168 YAML files, the pilot manifest, image sizes and README; 27 targeted
tests and Python compilation passed. No PDF was downloaded. There are now 388
capacitor renders; other capacitors and later families remain.

## Generic 8 x 6.5 mm SMD polarity check (2026-11-19)

The installed KiCad 10 `Capacitor_SMD:CP_Elec_8x6.5` footprint has pad 1 at x = -3.05 mm and pad 2 at x = +3.05 mm. The polarity indicator in the KiCad model still needs its spatial position confirmed against pad 2; do not publish the example on the footprint observation alone. Preview session 34618 remains active. Search for an exact 8 x 14.5 mm 680 uF / 16 V manufacturer match did not yield a reliable result; do not assume an 8 x 15 mm product is exact.

For the unsourced 8 x 14.5 mm / 680 uF / 16 V record, Yongming's VKM catalogue (https://www.ymin.cn/chip-miniature-type-aluminum-electrolytic-capacitors-vkm-product/) lists VKMD1251C681MV as 8 x 12.5 mm / 680 uF / 16 V and VKMD1451C821MV as 8 x 14.5 mm / 820 uF / 16 V. Neither is an exact match. This is negative evidence only; do not use either as the represented part without further review.

## IC preparation: Nexperia 74HC00 package-specific markings (2026-11-19)

While the capacitor jobs run, identified two existing source records for a future IC pilot. The repository records match the manufacturer, orderable numbers and respectively `SOIC-14_3.9x8.7mm_P1.27mm` and `TSSOP-14_4.4x5mm_P0.65mm` footprints. Nexperia links separate package 3D models and dimensions on the same page. These strings must not be interchanged across packages. Exact lettering style, line placement and production date/lot are not established by this table; do not invent them. Source STEP availability, dimensions, material regions and pin-one orientation still need inspection. This is preparation only; finish the capacitor sequence before IC rendering. No PDF or 3D file was downloaded.

For the future Nexperia 74HC00 SOIC-14 and TSSOP-14 pilots, the installed KiCad 10 package models exist at `Package_SO.3dshapes/SOIC-14_3.9x8.7mm_P1.27mm.step` (SHA-256 `3b9a775da256c8afcc28dcddfbc2eb3f266107fe61ce8446d96f2c52b6f44a70`) and `Package_SO.3dshapes/TSSOP-14_4.4x5mm_P0.65mm.step` (SHA-256 `3ee5786aae56190997aa793ba3cfc003fd616782770e29719b4562f012982245`). Presence and hashes do not prove package correctness, face colours, pin-one placement or top marking orientation; inspect those before IC rendering.

Read-only STEP root/bounds/colour inspection for the two future Nexperia 74HC00 IC models is running in session 48845. Capture its results as preparation only; pin-one and marking orientation require a separate explicit check, and capacitor publication remains first.

The IC STEP inspection completed: both models have one root. SOIC-14 total bounds are 6.0 x 8.7 x 1.75 mm; TSSOP-14 total bounds are 6.4 x 5.0 x 1.0 mm. Each has 54 dark-body faces (`0.148,0.145,0.145`), 182 metallic faces (`0.824,0.820,0.781`), and one pin-indicator-coloured face (`0.691,0.664,0.598`). For the future IC pilots, raw STEP pin-indicator centres are SOIC (-1.1102, +3.5102) mm and TSSOP (-1.3703, +1.6703) mm. Corresponding KiCad PCB pad 1 positions are (-2.475, -3.81) and (-2.8625, -1.95) mm. KiCad model/PCB coordinate transforms and orientation of projected lettering must still be explicitly verified before publication; these raw values alone are not proof of alignment.

Green/gold GR 8 x 12 mm 470 uF / 10 V preview is running in session 71738 at `tmp/display_render/radial_gr_8x12_470uf_10v_preview`. Inspect its polarity, colour and marking before the final.

For both proposed 74HC00 KiCad footprints the installed model offset and rotation are zero and scale is one. Applying the usual PCB-versus-Cartesian Y inversion places both STEP indicator centres in the same quadrant as PCB pad 1. KiCad's developer documentation describes the PCB Y-axis inversion at https://dev-docs.kicad.org/en/import-formats/pads/ . Treat this as a coordinate-based alignment check, not a substitute for inspecting pin-one and text direction in a rendered image.

Nexperia's [SOT108-1 outline](https://assets.nexperia.com/documents/package-information/SOT108-1.pdf) gives SO14 package length 8.55–8.75 mm, lead span 5.8–6.2 mm and seated height at most 1.75 mm; the KiCad model's overall bounds are 8.7 x 6.0 x 1.75 mm. Its [SOT402-1 outline](https://assets.nexperia.com/documents/package-information/SOT402-1.pdf) gives TSSOP14 length 4.9–5.1 mm, lead span 6.2–6.6 mm and seated height at most 1.2 mm; KiCad bounds are 5.0 x 6.4 x 1.0 mm. These comparisons support the general scale only; body dimensions, marking placement and representative real-world height still need individual review. No PDF was downloaded.
